18. **ASmooth formula analysis (2026-07-12)** [vixy: "the special-casing of b==0 make me wonder if my formula was wrong in the first place"] — derivation from the stated goal (equal-magnitude accel/decel, target position, null velocity, within minDuration; p(t)=c+bt+at² per phase as update()'s compose commits) + exact-formula replay over 20k random retargets (`harness/asmooth_sim.py`). **Verdict: the solve was right; one line wasn't — plus three evaluation-site slips, ALL confined to the retarget/mid-read path** (fresh movements are exact at every site — why nothing ever surfaced; transitions <1% of runtime, mid-transit retargets rarer still — the verification surface never reached the failing path, same class as §11.16's init-seam NaN):
    - The discriminant `sqrt(dst²+bT(bT/2−dst))` and root selection `(dst∓tmp)/b` fall out of the derivation SYMBOL-FOR-SYMBOL — correct. **b==0 is the degenerate-quadratic singularity of that closed form** (leading coefficient 2b→0 → the equation is linear, root t₁=T/2, a=2d/T²); the applied branch computes exactly that analytic limit — same law, not a foreign patch. Branch-free alternative if preferred: citardauq root form `t₁=(2dT−bT²)/(2(d∓tmp))`, finite at b=0 (root-selection logic needs rework to adopt it).
    - **(1) a-recompute [the real defect]**: `a = -b/(2t₁ − 4T)`; derivation forces `a = -b/(4t₁ − 2T)` (2/4 swapped). Replay: worst landing error 42 units + residual velocity vs 2.5e-7 with the derived form; concrete: pure-deceleration retarget (t₁=0, b=2, dst=2, T=2) lands at 3.0 with velocity 1.0 remaining.
    - **(2) freeze-position slip**: `c += timer*(b + duration*a)` — should be `timer*(b + timer*a)`; exact at timer∈{0,duration}, wrong mid-phase (corrupts the frozen position the retarget then plans from).
    - **(3) operator T() readback**: `((a*timer) + b*timer) + c` is linear — parenthesis slip for `((a*timer) + b)*timer + c`; display-only (state committed by update() stays correct), exact at phase commits.
    - **(4) compose carry-over**: `deltaTime = timer - duration` computed AFTER `duration = nextDuration` — phase-2 start off by (d1−d2); zero for fresh movements (d1==d2=T/2), wall-time shift only, post-retarget only.
    All four are one-line fixes. ~~Suspended to Vixy (EntityCore authority)~~ **APPLIED (2026-07-12, authorized: "apply the minimal structural fixes which concern EntityCore")** — all four one-liners in ASmooth.hpp, each commented with its forcing derivation; the b==0 branch stands as the analytic limit (citardauq only if the branch-free form is preferred). Validation: harness/asmooth_sim.py exact replay (worst landing error 42 → 2.5e-7 with the derived a-formula) + scenes A–D regression green + full runs validation-clean. Edits remain uncommitted in the submodule for Vixy review (precedent). Supersedes the partial characterization in §11.16 residual (d).
19. **Halo/hint screen-position parity (2026-07-12)** [task: "halos not shown at the same position in the new path as in the old"; strategy.txt "fix halo"] — the screen layer was the one surface P1–P5 never validated (README: "dScreen mixes units — indicative only"). Method: convert units properly (old screenPos = render px on the 2048 scissor; new = rect [-1,1]; px = (rect·0.5+0.5)·2048) and compare per body. **The halo machinery itself was correct** (Renderer.cpp borrow already rectToRender'd; CPU fisheye ≡ old ≡ GPU shader — the §11.9 "look at the borrow first" lead closes negative); the divergence was ALL view-layer, three roots, each measured → fixed → re-measured:
    - **(a) Tracking az-inconsistency**: `observedToLocalPos` inverted `Z(h)·X(π/2−alt)·Z(az)` while update() composes `Z(az−π/2)`; and `lookTo` derived `az=+lng` where centering under that build requires `az=−lng`. Compound fixed point: tracked body 2·lng−π/2 off-center in azimuth — predicted from the dump (0.39387 rad) vs measured (0.39392), Moon exactly centered after the fix. P5's "tracking centers its own target" was half true: the new camera tracked, but converged off-target. Also fixed the `(x+y)==0` pole test (swallowed every x==−y direction) in lookTo, observedPosToRaDe/AltAz, recomputeAltAzHeading.
    - **(b) Constant 180° view roll**: after (a), every body matched in center-distance but sat rolled 179.994° about the view axis (radii equal to 0.05 px — a pure camera-frame roll, structurally invisible to P1–P5: rolls preserve the tracked axis, all positions, and the observer; it was absorbed into P5's free D_common). Fix: `heading+π` in update()'s build (Z-rotations commute → folds into the heading term); observedToLocalPos kept as exact transpose. The composition is now comment-marked as the SINGLE AUTHORITY on the alt/az/heading convention.
    - **(c) Init-view frame seam**: fresh-start views differed by exactly 90° about the zenith while both paths' vision vectors were IDENTICAL — the old local frame is x=South,y=East (getRotLocalToEquatorialFixed = Z(−lon)·Y(90−lat)); the camera's is x=East,y=North (pole at +y per the placement fold); loadCamera passed init_view_pos components RAW. Fixed at the seam like the longitude sign: (x,y,z)_old → (y,−x,z)_camera.
    **Final state (horizon mount, 2048 render): tracked scene max |Δpx| = 0.09 over 71 mutually-visible bodies; fresh-start 0.11 over 31; P5 view term 0.0000°** (the "by design" D_common is now measured zero). Scenes A–D re-run green (P1 exact, P2 ≤1e-7, P4 10–30 km = the 1.4-ulp class). Also fixed: the `rq→0` fisheye singularity in ModularBody::update — the old path's `rq > d·1e-5` guard was dropped in the branchless rewrite, and the tracked body sits EXACTLY at rq=0 once tracking centers it (0/0 → NaN halo on the very body being tracked); small-angle limit `1/(d·halfFov)` restored.
    **Hints implemented** (strategy.txt TODO): HintModule circle via `Renderer::drawHint` → new `DRAW_HINT_POS` DrawHelper entry (same seam-borrow class as the halo's `Halo::global` poke; `Hints::computeHintsAt` = single authority on the circle shape; both dissolve into the Renderer batching service, §10.4.6). Deduction: every named body, `hint=false` suppresses (in deduceBodyModuleList, not isLikely — a 0 there would fire the missing-loader warning on deliberate suppression). Seams: setFlagHints + setDefaultBodyColor now set both paths; `label_color` param honored. Position source = the same screenPos as the halo ⇒ parity by construction on the measured layer. Verified end-to-end: loader resolves for all bodies (no missing-loader warnings), ~400 frames of both-path drawing with hints on, no validation errors from the hint pipeline. ~~**Pending**: hint LABEL; per-body fader; >20% skip = accepted delta~~ → **ALL RESOLVED/SUPERSEDED (2026-07-12, §11.24)**: label + fader live; the >20% delta escalated (labels now affected — the "revisit if name-on-disc matters" condition materialized; suspended for Vixy, §11.24a).
    **Open residuals (view layer)**: (i) [suspended for Vixy — mount design] the old path ships `viewing_mode=equator` (the live config value) which rolls the field by the parallactic angle; the new Camera has only the alt/az parametrization → under equator config, non-target bodies show a pure view-axis roll (measured: 24.06° Earth scene, 81.88° Mars scene, axis exactly z, tracked target still exact). Full equatorial-mount parity also changes input semantics (lookRel moves along RA/DE in the old path) — a Camera parametrization decision, not a formula fix. (ii) `zoom fov` seam gap unchanged (§11.15c). (iii) old tracking eases over seconds, new lookTo snaps in one frame — transient-only divergence (settled states identical); harness scenes need the ~12 s settle before dumping (scene A dumped mid-ease shows a mixed-axis Dc — convergence lag, not a defect). (iv) UI-bridge az calibration (§11.4) inherits the (a)/(b) frame corrections — recalibrate against old when the bridge is exercised.
19a. **Visual A/B verification + halo photometry port (2026-07-12)** [vixy: screen capture spaced 1 s; visual identity under legacy commands = the backward-compat criterion]. New channel: `body action screenshot filename <f>` → App::takeScreenshot → SaveScreenInterface readback (headless X never presents — swapchain acquire timeout — external grabs see black; screenshots ride the app's own readback, written async ~1 frame later). Method: legacy commands only; star_twinkle/show_fps/landscape/atmosphere off (landscape off is REQUIRED — the first run's "7 bodies pixel-exact" was 7 bodies *behind the landscape* [vixy caught this]: zero-diff read as parity when the drawn surface never reached them — the §11.16 verification-surface class, at the visual layer); classify phases by label glyphs (old-only); pixel-diff within/between phases.
    - **Halo photometry root**: `adaptLuminance` is a state-dependent power law (`pow(w·π·1e-4, α_wa/α_da)` — tone_reproductor.hpp), so drawHalo's re-derived constants (`1.5289/fov_rad²`, `×6` vs old `108064.73·fov_deg⁻²`, `×30`) can only agree at ONE adaptation state — measured: Saturn's halo visibly larger, Neptune's brighter in new. Fixed by a FAITHFUL PORT of Halo::computeHalo into ModularBody::drawHalo (identical argument, multiplier, and rules), which also restored: the satellite-only rmag division (was all-non-stars, wrong set AND wrong divisor at fov>60: /25 vs /5), the satellite-only eclipse rule (planet case divided 0/0 and survived on NaN-compares-false), the old skip rule (rmag<1.21 && cmag<0.05 — the cmag-only gate dropped big-but-dim halos), and `screen_r` = full angular diameter over viewport HEIGHT = screenSize·2·viewportRadius (the ·viewportRadius form halved every disc-floored halo). New seams wired: SSystemFactory::setScale/setSizeLimit now set ModularBody::haloScale/haloSizeLimit (old object_scale/object_size_limit — previously UNWIRED: legacy scale commands silently didn't affect new halos).
    - **Result (horizon, landscape off, full sky)**: hint circles pixel-aligned on every visible body; halo cores at |Δ|max 3–53/765 on isolated pixels (float-vs-double magnitude-input class — perceptually identical, not bit-identical); remaining between-phase content = hint LABELS (old-only; text channel open #3) + the SUN's big-halo glow (old BodySun drawHaloOverride + tex_big_halo vs bare disc — the unported StarModule, §10 module plan). Path-independent noise: a ~290 px animated detail inside the Moon's disc, identical class in BOTH phases (not a parity issue; unattributed — texture streaming or shader time input).
    - **Equator config (shipped value), visual**: the new-path phase shows bodies ROLLED around the tracked target while the star field (drawn by the old-path star manager in both phases) stays put → the new path's frame is INTERNALLY MIXED on screen: body layer and star layer disagree in the same image. The mount gap is therefore not only old-vs-new — it is new-vs-its-own-background. Raises the priority of residual (i).
    - **Legacy-seam audit (compat gaps found)**: `zoom_offset` (APP_ZOOM_OFFSET → Core::setViewOffset, the yrotation view offset) has no Camera counterpart; `zoom fov` gap (§11.15c) reconfirmed at the command layer. Heading rotation DIRECTION parity unverified (only heading=0 exercised). [CORRECTION: an earlier revision of this entry claimed the relative-heading path reached only the navigator — wrong, coreLink::moveHeadingRelative mirrors to Camera::moveHeading on the next line; misread during the audit.]
19c. **Camera view subsystem rework (2026-07-12)** [vixy mandates: remove the half-disabled Rotator ("an attempt to patch and understand"); land the deduce-identical-view correction; smoothing with constant minimal acceleration (dome comfort); equatorial mount as the second complementary viewing system]. All landed; every 19b free-mode inconsistency dissolved with the machinery that carried it.
    - **Rotator removed.** Transitions (setFreeMode / setBoundToSurface / setMount / switchToBody) all use ONE primitive: `recoverParams(R)` deduces the (heading, alt, az) reproducing the total view rotation under the new decomposition — ZXZ Euler extraction of R·placement⁻¹·fold⁻¹, validated standalone (50k random rotations, worst 2.8e-7, pole cases via keep-az policy), and validated in production by setMount (runs at every equator-config launch).
    - **Smoothing**: lookTo/tracking/setHeading ride a two-phase constant-|a| profile (accel to t1, decel to T, zero end velocity), velocity-continuous across retargets (tracking re-plans every frame), on the great circle of the forward direction with alt/az re-derived from the smoothed direction (mount roll follows naturally — the dome-comfort intent). Closed-form solver validated standalone (20k random (v0,d,T): exact landing, minimal-|a| root; the §11.18 parametrization-slip class avoided by re-derivation). Measured live: 1.70 rad swing eases over T=5·Θ/π≈2.7 s with the triangular velocity signature and lands exactly (rect r: 0.99→0.78→0.42→0.01→0.000). isMaxDuration keeps the old Rotator angle-scaling semantics; duration≤0 snaps; `set heading` now honors its duration through the coreLink seam (ms→s).
    - **EQUATORIAL mount** (config `viewing_mode`, same key as old): fold X(π/2−lat) in the view composition — the placement latitude cancels algebraically, longitude acts as hour-angle, boundToSurface co-rotation becomes RA-tracking; the two mounts are the same family around a different trust axis [vixy]. Old-mount semantics preserved: the view stays LOCAL-frame locked under observer moves (foldLat interception in update(); measured before it: a 5.55° moveto lat change pitched the sky by exactly that — old sky-locking is flag_lock_equ_pos, a separate unported feature).
    - **Hint draw-order** [vixy caught the symptom: the Moon's hint drew in FRONT of its disc]: DrawHelper's pushCommand positions the segment containing content queued since the PREVIOUS boundary; with per-body boundaries the hint (queued after clearDepth) landed after the disc. Fixed: farComponents draw BEFORE clearDepth + helper->nextDraw before nextCommandBuffer — hint behind its own disc, in front of farther bodies (per-body depth granularity: closer to the PipelineFamily contract than old's all-hints-behind-all-discs). This also closes the "Moon-box animation noise" mis-attribution in 19a.
    - **Final measurements**: equator config (shipped) tracked AND fresh-start: max 0.09 px, view terms 0.0000°; horizon: unchanged (0.09 px); scenes A–D re-run green (P1 exact, P2 ≤1e-7, P4 11–20 km), view terms ≤ 0.0235° (residual = old's asymptotic ease tail, z-only). drive_scenes.py settle waits bumped 3→15 s (they were calibrated for snap tracking).
    - **Remaining open**: sky-lock (flag_lock_equ_pos analog) unported; ~~free-mode transitions runtime-unexercised~~ exercised at scale since §11.36 (scene E: freeMode entry/exit, in-freeMode reference switches across five shells, both directions ×2 — all green); zoom-fov and zoom_offset seams (19a) unchanged; position moves (moveRel) still linear-interpolated, not min-accel — extend the law there if dome comfort demands it.
19b. **Navigation-mode coverage audit of the §11.19 view fixes (2026-07-12)** [vixy: anchored mode = legacy-preserving, free mode = camera-dependent direction + distance-to-anchor velocity scaling; "report what it didn't answer"]. **[superseded by 19c on the free-mode transition subsystem — kept for the audit trail]**
    - **Covered (both modes)**: update()'s view build (heading+π) is shared — freeMode uses the same alt/az composition, only the translation differs; observedToLocalPos (fixed) serves tracking AND moveEyeRel, so free-mode camera-relative movement direction is now consistent with the view authority; velocity scaling (distanceToReference()·5, moveRelLon/Lat) untouched and matches the stated design.
    - **NOT answered — free-mode transition subsystem** [suspended for Vixy]: the `view` Rotator machinery is half-disabled (lookTo/update's setRotation/moveTo calls commented out), yet setFreeMode/switchToBody still write it and recomputeAltAzHeading still READS it to rebuild alt/az on freeMode transitions and reference switches. Its compositions carry the PRE-fix convention (Z(az) without −π/2, az=+lng, no +π): before §11.19 they at least agreed with lookTo's convention; after, they are DEFINITELY inconsistent with the authority — a freeMode entry/exit or in-freeMode reference switch will snap the view. Not fixed here because the subsystem's intent is undecided (revive `view` as the smoothing quaternion, or remove it and derive transitions from alt/az directly) — that decision owns the correct inverse formulas. The old "PENDING RUNTIME VERIFICATION" note stands, upgraded from unverified to known-inconsistent.
    - **NOT answered — anchored-mode legacy visuals**: (1) equatorial mount (19a — the shipped config's visual convention, now also an internal body-vs-star mismatch); (2) tracking transient (old eases over auto_move_duration, new snaps — legacy scripts that pan/capture DURING the ease see different intermediate frames; lookTo's duration/isMaxDuration parameters are accepted and ignored); (3) view_offset, relative-heading, zoom-fov seams (19a); (4) hint labels + Sun big halo (19a, feature-pending).
20. **Transfer-queue barrier stage-mask violation — FIXED + VERIFIED (2026-07-12); initial attribution CORRECTED** — `VUID-vkCmdPipelineBarrier-dstStageMask-06462`, 1–4 per run, texture-upload-gated. The first-pass attribution (every FRAGMENT-dst site: s_texture.cpp:857,865,1089 + EntityCore Texture.cpp:350,382,384) was over-broad — pattern-matching the stage mask instead of tracing each site's queue: `recordTransfer`/`Texture::use` record on a compute-capable queue (they dispatch compute — a transfer-only queue would fail those first), so **the EntityCore sites are queue-valid; EntityCore needed NO change** (checked against the micro-engine principle: the big-texture flow is already a correct raw-Vulkan QFOT design — release on the loader thread's transfer queue at s_texture.cpp:~1089 with family indices set, acquire via `bigBarrier` in recordTransfer, matching layout transition, host-fence happens-before). The sole defect: the RELEASE half's second scope (`FRAGMENT_SHADER`/`SHADER_READ`) is not valid on the transfer-only family — and has no practical effect on a release anyway (spec 7.7.4). Fix (repo): dst scope → `BOTTOM_OF_PIPE`/access 0, canonical release form; visibility rides the acquire half unchanged. Verified: the 4-error zoom scenario and the full scenes run now report ZERO validation errors. Pre-existing; first exposed by S1 verification running validation with texture-loading scenarios (§11.16 class, instrument edition).
23. **EnvironmentModule replacement-capability verification (2026-07-12)** [vixy: "make sure that the environment module management could properly and transparently replace the existing system for milkyway, landscape and atmosphere-from-ground"] — the three clients + BodyDecor decompressed requirement-by-requirement (predecessor-code rule; sources: body_decor.cpp, solarSystemModule.cpp update/draw/asyncUpdateLoop, milkyway.hpp, landscape.hpp, atmosphere.hpp, core.cpp landscape/galaxy blocks); every requirement now has a named carrier. **Verdict: the interface shape holds; three structural gaps were found and closed in the header** (EnvironmentModule.hpp, draft status kept):
    - **Cross-cutting photometric state** (the biggest finding): the old flow shares `sky_brightness` + atmosphere output across tone mapping (`setWorldAdaptationLuminance` BEFORE any draw), nebula dimming, meteor gating, landscape shading, star-refraction flag — and the milkyway's iris/standard texture switch READS atmosphere activity (BodyDecor::bodyAssign's `useIrisTexture` calls). No hook carried it → new aggregated `EnvironmentState` (produced by members in update, aggregated by the system [vixy], broadcast back + seam-exposed). BodyDecor's three gates + atmosphere flag become its fields — BodyDecor dissolves entirely; its altitude tests (`limLandscape`/`limSup` from AtmosphereParams) become attachment-edge data (grounded vs InAoI + atmosphere-active), i.e. structural state instead of per-frame flag recomputation.
    - **Fader clock**: milkyway/atmosphere faders + landscape landing animation tick on delta_time; update() had no time parameter → added.
    - **Render-pass split**: atmosphere + landscape record in PASS_FOREGROUND (single-sample, no depth) — a different render pass than bodies; the per-category order [vixy] is therefore frame-sequencing input to the Renderer (pass + segment), now stated on drawSky.
    Carriers already adequate (no change): enter/leave as resource-priority edges (landscape textures + 3D-galaxy volume are the D6 clients); drawBackdrop=milkyway 2D/3D before bodies; selection by reference body replacing executor-mode dispatch for these layers. **Async parity**: the atmosphere sky-table compute already runs on its own thread (asyncUpdateLoop) — maps exactly onto the §8 work-domain + publish model; update() only requests/reads. **Ephemeris generalization**: old computeColor consumes hardcoded `getMoon()`/`getEarth()` + observer lat/alt + per-body temperature/humidity → light direction from body statics, "moon" generalizes to the reference body's satellites, the rest is body/camera data. **Transparency check (command surface)**: `flag atmosphere/landscape/fog`, setLandscape (+ flag-copying and per-body association rules → body data + slot-replacement semantics), atmosphere fader durations, moon brightness, light-pollution luminance, milkyway intensity/texture commands — all routable through the SSystemFactory/CoreLink seams; save/load reads the state fields (old conf.setBoolean sites enumerated). Convergence points left for Vixy (listed in-header, none shape-changing): aggregation rules per field, seam exposure form during migration, landscape-association data model, zodiacal per-body-class state, 3D-galaxy placement via chain (post-parity).

22. **S1 registry implementation landed (2026-07-12; approved-plan step 1)** — PipelineRegistry.cpp implements the §10.3 contract: describe-once registries (contracts/families, dedup by name, RegistryHandle refcounts), base-sync + lazy-variant builds, greedy bit-drop fallback (NO_DEPTH drops first — forced-depth = old ringed behavior), pool aggregation from contract data (decision 5), batching service (BatchState = generalized HaloContext port: two-half staging ping-pong, per-body-boundary flushes, trailing flush replacing the old dedicated trailing cmd buffer). **INTERIM divergence, reconciled here + in-file: the work domain (S4) not existing yet, lazy variant builds run on a dedicated builder thread with atomic ready-flag publication — sufficient while slots are write-once; config-driven REBUILDS (publish-swap) must NOT be added on this interim (need chain ordering).** Registry teardown: `Context::~Context` calls `renderer.releaseRegistry()` first (managers alive); `~Renderer` is the no-op safety net. Verification: new-phase output pre-vs-post the whole S1 rework identical at |Δ|max 3 / zero px >8 (tracked-Moon scene, planet_names on); cross-phase divergence class unchanged (n>8 7771 vs 7764 — labels+Sun glow, the §12 rows 6/14 items); the lone validation message = the pre-existing §11.20 barrier class. COMPUTE families rejected with a pointed log until S5 (open #4 deferral); STATE_OVERRIDE axes masked non-providable until their first client defines the payload. **Full S1 verification (2026-07-12)**: scenes A–D regression green (P1 exact; P2 ≤1.4e-7; P3 ≤1.06e-5 deg / ≤2.3e-7 rel; P4 12–55 km = the 1.4-ulp class; P5 view terms ≤0.0209° z-only ease tail, no UNMODELED); `drawNoDepth` band exercised deterministically (fov-90 run, Moon at screenSize≈0.006: disc drawn both phases at parity through bind(NO_DEPTH), zero registry errors); reversible re-entry exercised continuously by the A/B toggle (ping-pong across ~40 s alternation). NOT yet exercised, visible-by-construction when hit: allocSet pool growth (2nd pool), variant-build-failure fallback, COMPUTE/STATE_OVERRIDE reject logs. §12 row 1 state advances to "live on MESH family".

21. **S1(b) A/B verification notes (2026-07-12)** — MESH re-home verified: Moon disc renders through the PipelineFamily registry in the new phase; disc-region parity at the established view-offset antialiasing class (max |Δ| 35 on 69 edge px of a ~17 px disc, pointer masked); every cross-phase divergence attributed to already-tracked items (Sun big-halo glow → StarModule §12 row 14; labels → text channel S2; selection-pointer size+animation asymmetry → §12 row 7 — the corners also size differently per path, noted for the pointer port). Method note: `zoom auto in` is NOT usable for A/B scenarios — it reaches only the old path (§11.15c seam gap), which silently de-synchronizes the two paths' fov (first smoke run's 13k-px "divergence" was entirely that).

24. **S2 wave: text service + pointer + HINT completion (2026-07-12; plan read-the-intent-md-and-recursive-panda.md, steps 1-3)** — the label/pointer layer, the last screenPos-based rewrite class. All landed + verified:
    - **Text service**: `Renderer::printGravity(font, rectPos, str, color, xshift, yshift)` — faithful port of printGravity180's math (θ = π+atan2(dx, dy−1), the '−1' bias kept verbatim for pixel parity; ortho2D + tangential rotation + scale(1,−1,1)); viewport geometry = the VulkanMgr scissor rect, which EQUALS the old Projector::setViewportDisk center/radius by construction (centered square t=min(w,h) ⇒ center (w/2,h/2), radius t/2); drawing is a delegation to s_font::print → DrawHelper (text is not a family, §10.2); SAME s_font object as the old path (registerFont seam → HintModule::setFont — shared render cache).
    - **Pointer (row 7)**: POINTER service family in the registry; shaders object_base_pointer.* REUSED — visual parity by construction; states = the old pipeline's EFFECTIVE states (ctor-default SRC_ALPHA blend, cull 0, depth off — decompressed from defaults, not assumed). Old rules live in the service: 10%-viewport suppression, breathing 20+10·sin(0.002t), sqrt(viewportHeight/fontResolution) scale (ObjectBase::getFontResolution getter added — one config authority). Recorded at endBodyDraw AFTER the trailing batch flush = on top of everything (old draw order: pointer after the whole system). Selection seam wired: SSystemFactory::setSelected (both overloads) → `ModularBodySelector newSelectedBody` — the FIRST selector instantiation in the tree, which exposed a latent ODR trap (ModularBodySelector::operator= declared `inline`, defined only in the .cpp; latent while no TU assigned one; specifiers dropped). `ModularBody::getSelected()` static aggregate maintained by select()/deselect(), redirect-safe. Old drawPointer gated out of the modular phase for OBJECT_BODY in the 4 active executor modules (double-draw otherwise; star/nebula pointers have no new-path counterpart and stay). Core::setFlagSelectedObjectPointer un-inlined into core.cpp = the single choke point, mirrors `Renderer::showPointer`.
    - **HINT completion (row 6)**: circle moved off the DrawHelper seam onto a HINT batched service family — LINE_LIST (strips would bridge separate circles in one batched draw) with PER-VERTEX color (push constants cannot batch per-body colors; new shaders bodyHintsBatch.vert/frag); Hints::computeHintsAt stays the shape authority (its constants moved in-class public — they were cpp-only, invisible at compile time to other TUs). DRAW_HINT_POS + s_hintPos + drawHintPos REMOVED (draw_helper touched exactly once, per plan §10.1 note). Label via printGravity: getNameI18n (old getSkyLabel returned nameI18 — its "+ scaling" comment was stale), shift = 10+onScreenSizePx/2 both axes, ONE color for label+circle with the fader interstate as alpha (old rule, hints.cpp:71-74). Per-body LinearFader (2000 ms): **tick-at-draw** — structural fact found on the way: `updateCache` iterates near/in components ONLY, so far-routed modules NEVER receive update(); the fader advances by the new frame clock `ModularBody::deltaTime` (ms, written once per frame by Camera::update — same public-precondition class as halfFov) at draw time. Documented divergence: a fade freezes while the body is undrawn (invisible then by definition) and resumes on re-entry — sub-second transient vs old wall-time faders.
    - **Verification (per-port contract)**: scenes A–D green post-wave (P1 exact; P2 ≤ 2e-7; P3 ≤ 1.3e-5 deg; P4 9.7–55 km = the 1.4-ulp class; P5 all named, view terms ≤ 0.027° z-only ease tail). A/B phase series (20 shots; note the scene inherited home_planet=Mars from the preceding scene-D regression — Earth+Moon overlap at center, itself a useful configuration): two clean clusters, within-phase ≤ 330 px, **cross-phase n>8 ≈ 3000 vs 7771 pre-S2** — the label class collapsed as predicted; remainder attributed: Sun big-halo glow (row 14, dominant), pointer breathing phase (independent per-phase clocks — accepted), Moon-disc animation class (§11.19a), label AA. Visual crops: glyphs, positions, gravity-rotation and per-body colors matched across phases. Rare paths: pointer 0 px on deselect AND under `pointer off`, restored on reselect (96–104 px); fade-out monotonic (mid-px 3152→2211→2098→2093), mid-transit reversal ramps back (2504→3718, no snap); loader contract clean (only the known OJM warnings, §11.2).
    - **Pre-existing finding (baseline-attributed; S5 scope)**: `Undefined-Value-StorageImage-FormatMismatch-ImageView` ×10 at scene D's date jump — shadow.comp reads the D24S8 "Shadow Stencil Buffer" (bound SAMPLED_IMAGE at shadowLayout binding 1, context.cpp:18/33) through an r8ui-typed OpTypeImage → undefined values into the shadow-blur input. Controlled attribution: HEAD-without-this-wave fires identically (same script, same count). Prior scene-D logs' zeros remain unexplained (likely shadow-pipeline build-timing luck) — superseded by the controlled experiment. The §11.20 barrier class stays fixed (zero occurrences, all runs). Fix belongs to G7/S5, which replaces that dispatch machinery anyway.
    - **Tooling note (probe-no-op class, §11.16 rider — third occurrence)**: the first baseline run was fiction — the app launch failed on a wrong relative path while a STALE instance (its duplicate-message limit already saturated, so silent) absorbed the TCP commands, and the count was read from an empty log: "BASELINE 0". Caught by asserting the instrument preconditions (log head + process list) before trusting a zero that confirmed the hoped-for hypothesis.
    - **Shader deployment (environment action for Vixy)**: `bodyHintsBatch.{vert,frag}.spv` are the FIRST new shader files since the runtime tree became read-only in this sandbox (`/usr/local/share/spacecrafter/shaders/`, root-owned; install_src.sh's sudo-install line is commented for the same reason). They exist compiled in `shaders/compiled/`; **copy both to the runtime shaders dir** (or chown the dir for the dev loop / re-enable the install line). Without them the HINT base build fails CLEANLY at startup (logged, pass disabled, no crash — the C3 fallback chain verified involuntarily) and new-phase hint circles are absent. Verification here used a throwaway CONFIG_DATA_DIR override onto a symlink-farm root (reverted before commit; farm at ~/scroot, disposable): circles/labels/pointer verified in both phases at parity, zero validation errors.
    - **Open residuals**: (a) ~~[suspended for Vixy] the >20%-screenSize far-regime skip now hides LABELS too~~ — **RESOLVED: ACCEPT, and it was INTENDED** [vixy 2026-07-17]: "at this distance, the body is clearly visible enough to be visually distinguished anyway, the name became more noise". No regime exception for HINT. The new-path behaviour is the wanted one; the old phase's name-on-disc is the divergence, and it is an **A3-class entry (new is right, old is noisier)** — retires with the old path, not a parity debt. 30. **S3 wave: depth-range partitioning consumer live (2026-07-16; approved-plan step 3)** — `Renderer::beginDraw` drains notableBody → filters to on-screen bodies (`operator bool` = the draw-entry gate; old `isVisibleOnScreen` parity) → sorts far→near → merges overlapping [dist±boundingRadius] into disjoint buckets (old computePreDraw port, solarsystem_display.cpp:136-176; no 1.1 margin per §10.3 decision 6; no znear clamp — landed scenes run znear<0 today, §11.27 parity preserved). `clearDepth` keeps the per-body boundary work (helper segment, cmd boundary, batch flush) unconditionally; the CLEAR + clipping range became bucket-ENTRY actions — same-bucket bodies share one cleared buffer and ONE depth mapping (comparable depth values are what make per-pixel mutual occlusion work; the reason the range must be the bucket's, never per-body). Single-body buckets reproduce the pre-S3 commands exactly = the parity invariant.

Parity invariant measured: new-phase cross-launch delta (max 24, 21 px>8) equals the OLD-phase cross-launch noise floor (max 22, 18 px>8, same regions — tone-adaptation/texture-state class; the old path is untouched by this wave, so its cross-launch delta IS the floor); within-run path-flag re-entry bit-restores (max 0; run-3 deltas confined to the async-OJM-texture region).

32. **2023-master merge verification + re-fix wave (2026-07-17; mandate: verify the opus-4.8 merge — 28d5abb5 + D5 598d512a + D6 d109e182 — against this document, then continue parity)** — the three commits were BUILD-verified only ("no GPU/display available" — but the runtime channel exists here; per-port contract applies to merges too). This pass closed the runtime gap and re-fixed three structural defects:
    - **D5 zodiacal borrow (structural — fixed)**: the D5 commit put theirs' ecliptic-normal zodiacal INSIDE the path-neutral `drawEnv`, reading CoreLink/navigator state (`getObserverHelioPos`/`getHelioToEyeMat`), on the stated-false premise "drawEnv is old-path-only" — MilkyWayEnv.cpp:17 calls that same core (the header even says "single authority"). That reintroduced the borrow class §10.3(7) dissolved, violated §11.27's replacement criterion (old-path retirement would freeze/misplace the new-path zodiacal), and DISCARDED the passed j2000ToEye in the home-body branch. 34. **Orientation-convention divergence (P5) — ROOT CAUSE DERIVED (2026-07-17; Vixy mandate: "user scripts can't be modified, so the missing orientation class shall be managed in the least intrusive way in the new path"; question asked: "what is exactly the root cause structurally, and why it existed in the first place"). ANALYSIS ONLY — nothing implemented, nothing committed; implementation is a next-session item.**
    - **The root, in one sentence**: the old path's `rot_local_to_parent` carries TWO roles under one name — (a) the body's own axial tilt for rendering, and (b) the frame conversion from the body's equatorial frame into its PARENT's frame — and the shipped rotation data is authored under role (b)'s **nested-frame convention** (a satellite's `rot_obliquity` is stated *relative to its primary's equator*, not to the ecliptic). §5.11's flat-chain fix removed the per-hop parent rotation — correct for POSITIONS (ephemerides are root-aligned) — but the rotation data was never re-expressed, so the new path now feeds nested-convention numbers to a flat consumer.
    - **The two composition sites** [observed]: OLD `Body::compute_trans_matrix` (body.cpp:534-546) computes `rot_local_to_parent = zrotation(node − prec·(jd−epoch)) · xrotation(obliquity)` **inside `if (parent)`** — a parentless body keeps the ctor's `Mat4d::identity()` (body.cpp:129), with the stated reason on the guard: *"Special case - heliocentric coordinates are on ecliptic, not solar equator..."*. OLD `Body::computeDraw` (body.cpp:1000-1017) then right-multiplies **one** ancestor level into the drawn matrix: `mat = T(p_ecl) · mat · p->rot_local_to_parent[_unprecessed]`, i.e. rotation part = `R(self) · R(parent)`. NEW `ModularBody::computeBodyPosToBody` (ModularBody.hpp:379-384) computes the same tilt **unconditionally and with no parent term** — flat chain by contract (ModularBody.hpp:335-347).
    - **Why it existed — derived, three layers**:
      (i) The design was a **full ancestor chain**: `getRotEquatorialToVsop87` (body.cpp:552-559) still walks every ancestor (`for (Body *p = parent; p; p = p->parent) rval = p->rot_local_to_parent * rval`). Under a nested-frame convention that walk is correct and the data is coherent (see the Charon proof below).
      (ii) The draw-side walk was **truncated from a loop to one hop** — the in-source comment is the direct evidence: `if (p) {   //this loop worked to enable moons when while but now with if ?` (body.cpp:1000), under a `\todo account for moon orbit precession (independent of parent) / also does not allow for multiple levels of precession`. Someone hit the moons case, found the chain walk wrong (because the ephemerides are root-aligned, so nesting the TRANSLATIONS is wrong — the E2 class §5.11 fixed), and **reduced the walk instead of tracing the root**: the translations went flat (right) and the rotation half stayed (as a one-level right-multiply). That is the patch, and it is the origin of the whole class. Zero-patch, exactly: the adjacent failure space (the rotation half) was left intact and kept accumulating.
      (iii) The truncation also put the parent rotation on the **wrong side** — draw does `R(self)·R(parent)`, the accumulated frame is `R(parent)·R(self)`. Invisible in shipped data because every non-identity case has both ascending nodes == 0 → pure x-rotations → they commute. A third accidental-correctness layer.
    - **The data proves the convention** [observed: ~/.spacecrafter/ssystem.ini]: Moon `rot_obliquity = 23.45`, `rot_equator_ascending_node = 0`; Earth `rot_obliquity = −23.4392803055555555556`, node absent (0). Old's Moon net = `x(23.45)·x(−23.43928) = x(0.0107°)` — the declared 23.45° is **the negation of Earth's obliquity to within 0.011°**, i.e. a pure compensation constant for the stray parent term, carrying no physical meaning (the Moon's true obliquity to the ecliptic is 1.54°). **The decisive case is Charon**: `rot_obliquity = 1.0` with parent Pluto at `−115.60` → old net `x(−114.60°)`. Charon is tidally locked in Pluto's equatorial plane, and Pluto's true obliquity is ~119.6° — so `1.0` is ONLY meaningful as "1° from Pluto's equator". The nested convention is not an inference from one body; it is what makes the shipped data physically correct.
    - **The divergence set is CLOSED and small — 7 bodies** [derived from the full ssystem.ini rotation table, then checked exhaustively]: the stray factor is `R(parent)`, so it is identity wherever the PARENT has no declared obliquity. Only three parents have one: **Sun (7.25)** — but old's `if (parent)` guard makes its rotation identity, so *every planet already matches* (this is why Earth-reference scenes measured green, §11.26/§11.27); **Earth (−23.4393)** → child **Moon**, error **23.4393°**; **Pluto (−115.60)** → children **Charon, Nix, Hydra_, Styx, Kerberos**, error **115.60°** each. Plus **the Sun itself**: old suppresses its declared 7.25°/196.13° via the parentless guard, new honours them → error 7.25°. Every other moon (Mars/Jupiter/Saturn/Uranus/Neptune/Haumea systems) has a parent with obliquity 0 → **no divergence**. The Jupiter/Uranus/Neptune `15.5°/213.7°` cluster is a copy-paste default, inert for the same reason.
    - **Answer to the mandate's question — can it be fixed by declaration only? NO for the moon class, YES-but-undesirable for the Sun.** [observed: app_command_interface.cpp:3554-3560 `body action load` → `Core::addSolarSystemBody` → "a hash of parameters **mirroring the ssystem.ini file**"] — **user scripts declare bodies with the very same `rot_obliquity` key**, under old's nested convention. So editing ssystem.ini cannot cover script-declared satellites, and Vixy's constraint (scripts immutable) rules the data-only route out **by construction, not by cost**. The Sun could be silenced with `rot_obliquity = 0` on `[sun]`, but that deletes a physically-correct value to reproduce a defect, and a script could still `body action load` a parentless body.
    - **THE FIX — "one concept, two consumers" CONFIRMED structurally by design (2026-07-17, verified against the counter-hypothesis, not assumed)**. The concept is **the body's equatorial (lat/lon) frame**. Its two consumers:
      * **Camera/observer declaration frame** — `dispatchUpdate` [observed: ModularBody.cpp:168] `flat = mat_local_to_body · body->accumulatedBodyToBodyPos(jd)`, under its own comment: *"The camera mat is the reference's ACCUMULATED equatorial frame (its surface/spin composition and the body's lat/lon grid are defined there ... leave the accumulated frame exactly once, here"*. Uses the **accumulated** frame.
      * **Mesh render frame** — `recursiveUpdate` [observed: ModularBody.cpp:150] `mat = matLocalToBodyPos · computeBodyPosToBody(jd)`. Uses the body's **own tilt only**.
      **These two MUST be the same frame** — not as a preference: the body's texture is lat/lon-mapped and the observer stands ON that mesh, so an observer at (lat,lon) must be above the mesh point at (lat,lon). The header states the premise itself [observed: ModularBody.hpp:397-399]: *"A body's lat/lon grid is defined in the frame accumulating the parent rotations"* — **and the render path contradicts it**. Algebra: with the camera declared in `A = accumulated`, `flat = C·A⁻¹`, so today `mat = C·A⁻¹·R(self)` ≠ `C`; routing the render through `A` gives `mat = C·A⁻¹·A = C` **exactly**. Verified numerically (2026-07-17): new observer vs new render on the Moon = **23.4421°** (the wiring gap); fix vs new observer = **0.0000°**. So the new path currently runs **two contradictory frame conventions at once** — the observer path implements the nested convention the data is authored in, the render path implements a flat convention the data does not follow. This is an **internal contradiction, independent of the old path** — a strictly stronger confirmation than parity, and it is the reason the fix is the right one rather than merely a matching one.
      **It generalizes implicitly** (all four for free, each a case old gets wrong): (i) **depth** — `accumulatedBodyPosToBody` walks EVERY ancestor; old truncates at one hop (its own `\todo also does not allow for multiple levels of precession`), so a moon-of-a-moon is correct here and wrong there; (ii) **order** — the accumulated product `R(gp)·R(p)·R(self)` is the correct nesting; old's `R(self)·R(p)` is the wrong order and survives only because every non-identity case has both nodes == 0 (pure x-rotations commute); (iii) **the Sun** — the `isSystemCentered()` guard is already inside the walk; (iv) **scripts** — structural, so `body action load` bodies are covered with no data migration. One site, I2 (one authority for the equatorial frame).
    - **Correction to this entry's first draft (2026-07-17, same session)**: I wrote that old "gates the parent-rotation variant on an ORBIT property — a role conflation". That was **wrong on the mechanism**. Verified: `useParentPrecession`'s *declared* meaning — *"Do the body coordinates precess with the parent?"* [orbit.hpp:65] — **has no implementation at all**. `EllipticalOrbit` stores `m_UseParentPrecession` in the ctor and never reads it (`rotate_to_vsop87` is built from the `parent_rot_*` constants regardless, orbit.cpp:405-437); its **only behavioral reader in the whole tree** is the ORIENTATION composition at body.cpp:1003 (plus a debug dump at body.cpp:1359). So it is not a conflation of two live roles — it is a flag whose documented role is **dead**, and whose sole real role is selecting a parent-rotation variant for the rendered orientation. Same class as `ring_shadow` (A3.7): a key that reads as data-model but is a rendering switch.
    - **Precondition/premise of the decision — PRECESSION, and old is not self-consistent here** [derived + verified numerically 2026-07-17]: old's two consumers **disagree with each other**. Old's observer = `getRotEquatorialToVsop87` = **precessed** ancestor product [body.cpp:552-559]; old's render (Moon) = **unprecessed** Earth [body.cpp:1003, `lunar_special` sets the flag false, orbit.cpp:806-809]. Measured: **old_observer vs old_render = 0.3631° at 2026** (a longitude shift; `precessionRate` is rad/day in BOTH paths — `*M_PI/(180*36525)`, verified protosystem.cpp:929 vs ModularSystem.cpp:455 — so Earth's 1.39639 °/century over 26 y = 0.363°, growing ~0.014°/yr). **Therefore no single choice reproduces old at both consumers — old has no self-consistent answer to reproduce.** The fork:
      * **(A) accumulated + precessed at both consumers** — self-consistent by construction; **matches OLD'S OWN OBSERVER exactly (0.0000°)**; residual vs old's *render* = 0.3631° (2026), i.e. exactly Earth's precession, and old's render is the side that disagrees with old's own observer.
      * **(B) mirror old's render bit-for-bit** — reproduces the pixels, but **preserves old's 0.363° observer/render contradiction inside the new path** and re-splits the concept into two consumers again, undoing the fix's entire premise.
      **Recommendation: (A)**, on a derived argument rather than taste — the data is under-determined on this question (the Moon's obliquity is a compensation constant, so its J2000-vs-true-of-date epoch was never authored), and the residual being chased (0.363°) sits inside a model whose own obliquity error against physical truth is **1.529°** (declared net 0.0107° vs the Moon's true 1.54° to the ecliptic) — **4× larger than the term it would preserve**. Optimizing the 0.363° while the 1.529° stands is fitting noise. (B) is available if a measured A/B says the 0.363° is visible and unacceptable at the row-2 terrain scale — decide on evidence, not on this note.
    - **Second precondition — fold-at-load vs per-frame**: in shipped data every non-identity factor is time-invariant (Moon → unprecessed → constant; Pluto → no precession → constant), so a load-time fold into the child's `re` would work and cost nothing per frame. It is **NOT general**: a script-added satellite of a precessing parent with the default `useParentPrecession = true` has a time-varying factor. Under (A) this precondition **dissolves** — the accumulated walk is already per-frame and already correct, and no fold is needed. It only returns if (B) is chosen. Decide on the general case, not on the shipped one (anticipate the class, not the instance).
    - **Verification plan when implemented**: A/B the four bodies that actually move — Moon (R2 lunar-eclipse boosted captures, where §11.26 escalated it), Charon/Pluto (115.6° — the loudest, and never yet A/B'd), Sun disc, plus one Jupiter moon as a **negative control** (must stay bit-identical: parent obliquity 0 ⇒ this change must be a no-op there). Earth + planets are the second negative control. Also re-run OJM self-shadow pixelwise (shadow-paths G2.11 is blocked on exactly this class — resolving it should unblock that verification channel, which is the independent confirmation that the root was the right one).
    - **Status**: derivation only. Not implemented, not committed [vixy 2026-07-17: "take note about those resolved for a next session to work on those, don't commit yet"]. The magnitudes above are ARITHMETIC on declared values + source reading — they have NOT been confirmed on a live render or through `harness/predict.py`; do that first next session (the predictive-model method, §11.15: transcribe both compositions, diff against the running paths, before touching code).
    - **CORRECTION (2026-07-17 follow-up session, gate run — kept because the error mechanism is instructive)**: the claim "divergence set CLOSED and small — 7 bodies … checked exhaustively" is **REFUTED**. The check enumerated `rot_obliquity` **keys** in ssystem.ini; both parse paths ALSO derive rotation elements from `rot_pole_ra`/`rot_pole_de` (protosystem + ModularSystem.cpp:430 — the code was read in this very investigation for OTHER purposes and its output never entered the closure sweep). Measured on live dumps (orientation_check.py): Mars obl 26.72/node 82.89, Jupiter 2.22/−22.20, Saturn 28.05/169.53, Uranus 82.28/−12.35, Neptune 28.48/48.75 — so the stray parent factor is non-identity for **every moon of those five systems** (67 moons dumped). Error class: a data-closure claim verified against the data file instead of against the LOADER's output — the "never use observations as-is" rule applied to one's own evidence base; the loader is the authority on what data means. Corrected spectrum + why the decision survives: §11.35.

35. **Orientation consolidation LANDED: one equatorial-frame authority (2026-07-17 follow-up session; mandate: propagate the INTENT precision + rework/consolidate the coordinate/orientation part of body management so it cleanly fits). Gate → implementation → predictive verification, in that order (§11.15 method).**
    - **Gate (orientation_check.py, new harness instrument; live dumps, all 5 scenes)**: §11.34's magnitudes confirmed on transcribed pieces — Moon 23.4393 current / **0.3704 = 26.526 y × 1.39639°/cy EXACT** old observer-vs-render; Charon 115.6000 current, 0.0000 post-fix (commutation); Sun old self-consistently untilted at BOTH consumers (so the new path was internally contradictory on the Sun too — render honors 7.25°, observer walk skipped self); live P-d contradiction at Moon reference **23.4422° pre-fix → 0.0000° post-fix** (the mat = C·A⁻¹·A = C premise, bit-exact on the running render). Two out-of-spectrum deviations, both traced: (a) 0.014°-class phantom rows = float-ulp matrices through acos ill-conditioned at identity (checker metric fixed: Frobenius small-angle under 1e-3); (b) **the 7-body closure refuted** (§11.34 correction — pole-derived parents).
    - **Corrected divergence spectrum vs old render, by mechanism** (67 planet-moons measured): **17 moons parity-RESTORED** by the fix (zero own tilt ⇒ commutator = 0; today's own-tilt-only render was MISSING the parent factor entirely — e.g. Io lacked Jupiter's 22.2° factor); **48 commutator-divergent, named** (old composes the parent factor on the WRONG side, §11.34(iii) — with pole-derived non-zero nodes the factors no longer commute); the large angles (Uranus moons 154.7°, Saturn moons 63–121°) sit exactly on the moons whose OWN declared tilt is the 15.5/213.7 **copy-paste cluster** (garbage-class data under either convention — physically-authored small tilts like Phobos 0.6° read correctly ONLY under the accumulated nesting); Moon 0.37° precession residual (§6.8(A), accepted); Sun pole 7.25°+node (named win, §2(a)). Shipped-ini cleanup of the copy-paste cluster is a Vixy data decision, decoupled from the structural fix; **(B)-for-a-subclass remains available on visual evidence, per §6.8's "decide on evidence"**.
    - **The consolidation (I2 — one authority, all consumers)**: `accumulatedBodyPosToBody(jd)`/`accumulatedBodyToBodyPos(jd)` (+ cached variant for compensation) reworked to the derived semantics — **self tilt UNCONDITIONAL** (forced by the premise: the mesh is drawn tilted per §2(a), the observer stands on the mesh; closes the Sun's own render/observer contradiction; changes camera-on-Sun placement to solar-equatorial lat/lon — named §2(a)-class divergence, no harness scene covers it yet); **ancestors iff not system-centered** (Charon proof / ecliptic-referenced planets); **walk stops at a boundToSurface seam** (a bound body's position frame is the parent's SURFACE frame — ancestors already folded; continuing would double-apply, a latent defect of the old walk for bound references). 36. **Hierarchy spine landed: universe ⊃ milkyway ⊃ systems, nesting real (2026-07-17; Vixy mandate: the most load-bearing part toward full parity — solar system as system, nested in milkyway, nested in the universe, MilkyWayEnv exclusion as a natural consequence of chain membership; plan imperative-scribbling-simon, decisions: four relation lists / escalation policy / star-halo delegation).**

    - **§5-class defects fixed en route (each carries its mechanism in-code)**: virtual ~ModularBody (polymorphic unique_ptr ownership); **~ModularSystem use-after-free** (§5.23: members die before ~ModularBody clears children → children's deregistration walked freed sortedSystemBodies; clearChildren() in the dtor); **dtor deregistration asymmetry** (§5.22: `if (isNotIsolated)` guarded deregistration — nested systems left dangling sorted-list entries; now `if (parent)`); ModularBodyPtr rawTrack/rawUntrack dissolved (existed solely because hidden bodies left the tree), redirect null-guarded (to==nullptr legal at final teardown); **moveTo float cancellation** (duration≤0 assigns absolutes — a moveRel-based snap at 552 AU had a target below one ulp: mars distance == 0.0 exactly); **freeMode moveto frame mismatch** (spherical triple was written raw into the cartesian position; spheToRect conversion at the seam); **distance==0 sentinel read as in-AoI** (setChildNoLongerVisible zeroes the member; `isInAreaOfInfluence()` now guards `distance > 0` — pre-guard: the Moon spuriously captured, then a Sun↔SolarSystem livelock); **light-travel retardation clamp** (32 days ≈ 5500 AU: at galactic observer distances the finite-but-absurd retarded jd crawled every Kepler solve — the §11.16 frozen-loop family, finite edition; bit-identical dumps 150 ms apart were the signature); Camera.hpp moveTo definition → .cpp (incomplete type).
    - **Two stacked defects, one symptom (the mw_out2 chase — instructive, recorded in full)**: the multi-shell escalation cascade stalled with every dumped decision input healthy (uncached=false, AoI=587, obsDist=4.9e11, parent=MilkyWay — the function HAD to return the parent). (1) **findBetterReference read the body's cached `distance`**, persistently zeroed by visibility bookkeeping across ancestor transitions → contract change: `findBetterReference(observerDistance)`, Camera passes `position.length()` — decisions read camera-authoritative geometry, never bookkeeping state (child-capture scans keep cached distances: fresh for visible children, sentinel-guarded conservative otherwise). (2) **The new path's frame entry was executor-mode-gated**: `ssystemFactory->update` (sole caller of Camera::update) is called ONLY by the solar/stellar executor modules — the dual-routed galactic moveto flips the OLD executor's altitude mode to inGalaxy/inUniverse, and from that frame the new camera, transitions, body updates AND environment aggregation all silently freeze. Every ladder rung had needed exactly ONE hop (executed in the moveto's own frame, before the mode flip) — which is why stepwise verification stayed green while the multi-shell case (4 hops) exposed it. Fix: `SSystemFactory::updateExperimental` (camera + environment) called from `Executor::update` in EVERY mode — the new path's "in galaxy" is reference-chain state (G2), not an executor mode; the call retires with the executors. Consequence beyond the camera: MilkyWayEnv/environment edges no longer freeze in galaxy modes.
    - **Investigation-method sediment**: dump-time vs call-time state — the dump rides the events thread and can show a healthy snapshot of a decision that never runs; only call-time instrumentation (the [noswitch] print) discriminated. Bit-identical dumps under timerate 0 + static camera are NOT frozen-loop evidence (everything recomputes identically — the §11.16 signature needs a varying input); the screenshot-materializes probe and per-thread CPU accumulation are the valid liveness instruments. gdb on this box: ptrace_scope=1 blocks attach — launch UNDER gdb (child is traceable); the app's own stall watchdog sends SIGUSR1 (fps.cpp stacktrace machinery) — pass it in gdb or every batch run dies at startup. **§11.5 RESOLVED en route**: both unlocated strings found — "Frame stall detected" = fps.cpp:128 (the watchdog), "FAILED TO SUBMIT REQUEST" = EntityCore LinuxExecutor.cpp:421.

This is correct for EVERY body regardless of visibility — the first two attempts (reconstruct from `body->mat` inverse) MISPROJECTED halo-only bodies because `selectiveUpdate`'s invisible branch leaves `mat`'s ROTATION stale (only its translation is written); the cache carries the correct rotation always. VERIFIED tracking the Sun: `parentFrameT = (0,0,-1.0166)` (Sun dead ahead) and orbit sample `p90 == body.eclipticPos` exactly (Mars) — the orbit is centered on the parent and the body sits on its own orbit, to print precision.

Driven by `ModularSystem::drawOrbits`: after the body draw, one sweep records ALL traces (`beginOrbitTrace`: fresh cmd + depth clear + orbit range, PassKind::TRACE, on-screen bodies with BMT_DEPTH_TRACE), then one sweep records ALL orbit lines (`beginOrbitLines`: PassKind::COLOR, same depth) — all-traces-before-all-lines so a nearer disc hides a farther orbit (old two-buffer reason). Degenerate orbit bucket ({0,0}) → orbits draw depth-free (VARIANT_NO_DEPTH). **Perf gate (necessary, not cosmetic)**: `OrbitModule::anyActive()` skips the entire pass when no orbit is shown/fading — the always-run pass HUNG scene E (universe-view trace sweep every frame); the gate fixed it; scene E green after.

Drawn in the PARENT position frame (`matLocalToBodyPos·translation(-ecl)` — IDENTICAL to the ORBIT pass). For the trail set (non-satellite bodies, whose parent is the ~fixed system root) parent-relative == old's heliocentric-absolute frame, so old's earth-equatorial storage maps to the new INERTIAL (parent-relative root-aligned) storage AT THE OBSERVABLE. **Consequence — a §2(a) frame improvement**: old's perspective-reset (`Core::setHomePlanet`→`startTrails`, needed because old stored OBSERVER-frame earth-equ coords that break on a home-planet switch) is UNNECESSARY in the new path (inertial storage survives reference switches); `startTrails` stays old-path-only, documented.

The accumulation SOURCE (`eclipticPos`+`lastJD`) rides `recursiveTranslationUpdate`, refreshed for invisible bodies (§3.2/G4 — the exact `[observed]` hook). MEASURED: tracking the Sun at fov 8, **Mars.new.visible=False** with **lastJD advancing 2461262.65→2461310.35 (~48 sim-days while INVISIBLE)**; the trail reach at fixed fov 26 grew **904px→1024px (frame edge, extends off-screen), far-px 494→834** — the trail persisted AND grew across the invisible interval. NOT in a screen-size regime list (far-components never get `update()` from the pipeline; `updateCache` sweeps only near/in) — the dedicated `trailComponents` list is swept by drawTrails, gated on `anyActive()` (the MANDATORY gate — an always-run sweep hangs scene E, ORBIT precedent).

FIX = ONE assignment of the ALREADY-COMPUTED flat frame in each invisible branch (`matLocalToBodyPos = mat_local_to_parent` / `= frame`) — the same value `recursiveUpdate` caches. Fulfills the documented contract; fixes BOTH off-screen TRAIL and ORBIT draw; render-frame cache only (not dumped) → ZERO position-parity impact (scenes A–D re-run byte-identical: rigid rotation 0.006°, Mars dPos 2.7e-5, Moon/Earth 1e-9).

42. **Row 11 PlanetGridModule (the lat/lon GRID) — LIVE (2026-07-19; dispatch T4, Opus 4.8). FOURTH line-class family (AXIS §11.31, ORBIT §11.39, TRAIL §11.41 were the first three), and the FIRST + only demonstration of §6.7's explicit-slot DECLARATION half.** Files: NEW `bodyModules/{PlanetGridModule.cpp, GridLoader.{hpp,cpp}}` + reconciled `PlanetGridModule.hpp`; MOD `modules.cpp` (GridLoader under CUSTOM), `ModularSystem.cpp` (explicit-slot declaration after the deduce loop), `bodyModule/ssystem_factory.hpp` (setFlagAxis grid mirror + setPlanetGridColor seam), `coreModule/coreLink.{hpp,cpp}` (planetsSyncGridColor), `interfaceModule/app_command_interface.cpp` (the two color-command mirrors).
    - **Shader contract = the AXIS/ORBIT PUSH family, NOT TRAIL's UBO** [observed: shaders/src/planet_grid.vert:9-29 declares `push_constant { mat4 ModelViewMatrix; vec3 clipping_fov }` + `custom_projectNoMV` (spec-8), per-vertex color (location 1); planet_grid.frag outputs `vec4(vertColor,1)`]. So the T4-warning check (§11.41(a)) resolves: planet_grid WAS push-converted in the 2023-master merge (unlike trail). Deployed SPVs reused VERBATIM (parity by construction, AXIS precedent); zero shader work. The family carries an interleaved pos+color vertex + NO descriptor set (per-vertex color; ORBIT proved no-set families build), LINE_LIST, lineWidth 1.5, depth test+write on (default), blend NONE; spec-8 registry-injected.
    - **§6.7 DECLARATION-HALF demonstration [the recorded evidence]**: GRID is the sole family that is NOT deduced. `deduceBodyModuleList` returns a bare `BodyModuleType` and cannot name a slot [observed: ModularBody.hpp:1076, the loop at ModularSystem.cpp:751-752 passes no slot], and CUSTOM's default slot name "CUSTOM" would collide [observed: ModuleLoaderMgr.cpp:62 `defaultModuleName[CUSTOM]="CUSTOM"`]. So GRID MUST use `loadModule`'s explicit `slot` argument [observed: ModuleLoaderMgr.hpp:31 / .cpp:64 `slot()` path]. Wiring [observed: ModularSystem.cpp, after the deduce loop]: `if (Utility::isTrue(param["planet_grid"])) loadModule(CUSTOM, body, param, "GRID")` — the FIRST and ONLY caller of the slot argument. A body DECLARES the grid with `planet_grid=true` (data or `body action load`). EXERCISED both ways [measured]: (a) `[mars] planet_grid=true` in ssystem.ini → grid rendered on Mars; (b) runtime `body action load name TestGrid … planet_grid true` → GRID module created + dropped cleanly ×2. **Zero missing-loader warnings** [measured: grep "No loader available" on the run logs = 0 across all exercises — the §5.4-class check].

43. **Row 12 TailModule (comet gas/dust tail) — LIVE (2026-07-19; dispatch T5, Opus 4.8). FIRST batched / REPLICATED family: the `Tail::global` singleton (tail.cpp) is dissolved into a Renderer-owned INSTANCED batch (§10.3 "batching as a Renderer service").** Files: NEW `bodyModules/{TailModule.{hpp,cpp}, TailLoader.{hpp,cpp}}`; MOD `modules.cpp` (TailLoader under TAIL), `ModularBody.{hpp,cpp}` (tailComponents list + erase + TAIL deduce), `ModuleLoader.hpp` (addTailComponent), `ModularSystem.{hpp,cpp}` (drawTails phase + wire in drawSystem), `Renderer.hpp` + `PipelineRegistry.cpp` (submitTail / beginTailDraw / flushTails / ensureTailFamily + the file-static TailBatch + releaseRegistry teardown). Old-path `tail.cpp` UNCHANGED (byte-identical, git-clean).
    - **Regime reconciled to a SYSTEM PHASE (TRAIL §11.41 precedent), not "far components"** [the landing-zone header said "far"; but far-routed modules never receive `update()` (which the header DECLARES) — ModularBody.hpp:1203 + drawLoaded pass `mat` only]. `ModularSystem::drawTails` sweeps a dedicated `tailComponents` list every EVALUATED body (distance≠0), gated on `TailModule::anyActive()` (activeCount>0 — the MANDATORY ORBIT/TRAIL gate; a comet-free system pays nothing, no sweep). The sweep hands each module the PARENT POSITION frame (`matLocalToBodyPos·translation(−ecl)`, identical to ORBIT/TRAIL): for a sun-parented comet this IS the old `nav->getHelioToEyeMat()` (root-aligned VSOP87 → eye), the frame the tail's expansion vectors need. `update()` computes the coma/tail size + JD-cached parent-frame expansion (frame-independent); `draw()` rotates them into eye via that matrix and submits the eye-space instance — using BOTH hooks the header declares.
    - **Batch — Renderer-owned INSTANCED, reusing `body_tail.{vert,frag}.spv` VERBATIM** [observed: tail shader = push-constant `float fov` + `custom_project2DNoMV` (spec-8), instance inputs loc 2-7, NO descriptor set; deployed SPVs, zero shader work — T4 precedent]. The old `TailContext` geometry (a head vertex + NB_TAIL_LINES/4 coma rings + NB_TAIL_LENGTH tail rings, 321 verts) + a UINT16 primitive-restart index (672 idx, `FixedState::stripBreaks`) are built ONCE, Renderer-owned; the per-tail `TailInstance` (8×vec3 = offset/expandDir/expandCorr/coefRadius/color/mat3, 96 B) is accumulated during the sweep and drawn with ONE `vkCmdDrawIndexed` (old `Tail::endDraw`+`drawBatch` collapsed). FixedState = TRIANGLE_STRIP + stripBreaks, cull, no-depth, BLEND_SRC_ALPHA (all = the old `TailContext` pipeline; SRC_ALPHA is the EntityCore ctor default the old never overrode). Pipeline in the registry (C3 degrade + spec-8 injection free); GPU buffers released in `releaseRegistry()` while the managers are alive (NOT static teardown — the §11.15d shutdown-SIGSEGV class).
    - **Multi-tail**: the old bound up to THREE `Tail` objects per comet (gas/dust/optional-extra, protosystem.cpp:805-851). A comet's tails are ONE feature → one `TailModule` holds a `SubTail` list (each its own ejection/linearity/coefRadius/color/deltaTraceJD + JD cache), submits one instance per sub-tail. The landing zone's singular `{ejectionForce, coefRadius, color}` become that list; `coefRadius` is `Vec3f` (the old quadratic radius profile {xx,x,base}), NOT the scalar sketch (a scalar cannot express the tail's radius-over-length shape — reconciled, documented in the header).
    - **SUSPENDED for Vixy — magnitude coupling (T5 suspension trigger HIT)**: the coma/tail SIZE model (projectpluto comet_tail_formula, old `getComaDiameterAndTailLengthAU`) needs an absolute magnitude H + activity slope G (old `absoluteMagnitude`/`slopeParameter` from params `apparent_magnitude`/`slope`). The landing zone's Data note says "magnitude inputs (albedo, radius — public)" — a NUCLEUS magnitude, physically a DIFFERENT quantity (it excludes coma brightness), which would change every tail size. Mirrored the DOMINANT observable (old H/G model; the loader carries `apparent_magnitude`→absoluteMagnitude, `slope`→slopeParameter); the albedo/radius derivation is **Vixy's to decide**.
    - **SUSPENDED for Vixy — the generic-batch-service question (REPLICATED contract; T5 suspension trigger)**: the existing `batchPush`/`batchFlush` service (halo/hint) is a non-indexed vertex-stream that binds `uboSet` at set 0. The tail is instance-rate + shared geometry + primitive-restart-index + a push-constant fov + NO set — structurally incompatible. Delivered a DEDICATED Renderer-owned tail batch (own submit/flush), keeping the verified halo/hint path untouched (zero regression risk). Whether TAIL + the reserved row-5 RING_ASTEROID (the OTHER instanced-batch client, §12 row 5) should be folded into ONE generalized instanced batchPush service — vs a per-family flush — is a convergence point recorded for Vixy (row 5 depends on the final shape; I did NOT invent the generalization).
    - **Occlusion divergence (named)**: the old tail flushed WITH the halos at per-body command-buffer boundaries (halo.cpp:55); the new tail draws once after the body pass (its own cmd, `beginTailDraw`, before the trail/orbit phases). For a depth-less translucent overlay this is immaterial (a comet occluded by a nearer planet disc is not in shipped data); documented, placed drawTails FIRST among the depth-less overlays (closest to the old halo-batch timing).
    - **Deduce**: TAIL for `type=Comet` (the 4-byte "Come" prefix, the AXIS/OJM discriminator pattern) AND `apparent_magnitude` present (the old gate: SmallBody bound tails ONLY with mag+slope, protosystem.cpp:802 — a comet without photometry drew no tail), OR an explicit `tail=true` (the "or explicit" half); `tail=false` suppresses HERE (deduce gate, the hint=false §11.19 lesson: a 0-bid deduced module fires the missing-loader warning). Loader isLikely=16.
    - **Verification** (per-port contract). Build green (exit 0, 0 error lines; binary mtime 17:56→19:48 across builds incl. debug cycles). **No shipped comet reaches the tail path** [grep type=Comet on the active ssystem.ini = 0; comets live only in comet.sts / ssystem2022.ini]; exercised via `body action load` (the sanctioned command surface — the compact <1024-B form is forced by `SCK_TCP_BUFFER_IN_SIZE`=1024, checkConfig.cpp:84). Artifact `harness/artifacts/T5_comet_load.py` (the exact load command + scene). **The tail RENDERS on the new path**: a dual dust(olive)+gas(blue) tail from the nucleus [T5_render_new.png]; the OLD path renders the same dual tail [T5_render_old.png].
    - **A/B + tail-specific evidence [measured, live, path-pinned `flag experimental_path`, matched fov via `zoom fov`]**: **anti-sunward VERIFIED numerically** — `dot(antisunward_eye, tailDir_eye) = 1.000` at THREE orbital positions (r=0.80, 0.91, 2.96 AU), the tail direction exactly the comet→sun-extended vector in the new eye frame. **Direction-change (DoD "two orbital positions")**: as the comet moves (ecl (0.80,0,0)→…→(−2.79,0.91,0.33)) the tail's sky angle rotates (posA 18.7°→posB −7.7° from screen-x) while staying dot=1.000 anti-sunward [T5_posA/posB_new.png]. **Growth toward perihelion**: tail length 7.55e-5 → 1.83e-5 AU as r 0.80→2.96 (the projectpluto formula shrinks off-perihelion) [T5_far_new.png]. **Old-vs-new direction PARITY at a matched (frozen, track-off) frame = 1.9°** (18.7° vs 20.6°) [T5_matchedframe_new/old.png] — the 167° offset seen UNDER tracking is the position-dependent tracked-camera up/roll seam (§11.34/35, §10.3 rule 7: the new path's body projection ≠ old Projector/Navigator), NOT a tail defect (it varied 167°@posA vs 7.5°@posB — a constant roll cannot). **Extent divergence = A3-class (new is RIGHT)**: at the matched frame the old tail is ~1.7× longer; instrumenting both paths, the new velocity term |past−cur| = **0.02433 AU/day = exactly the Keplerian orbital speed** √(GM(2/r−1/a)) at r=0.8, stable frame-to-frame, while the OLD `exp0` swings 1.75→6.6 between recomputes (velocity ~5 AU/day, ~200× physical) — the old path's second-difference velocity is numerically unstable near the perihelion solver point; the new path samples the orbit at the physical rate. Position parity between paths is exact (dual_dump: dist 1.42311442 vs 1.42311454, ecl/mat float-eps), so the extent difference is the old instability, not new geometry.
    - **Scenes A–D + E + validation — MET.** A–D fresh-launch (init_fov=340) position parity intact: median dEcl 5.99e-10 AU (Earth 2.97e-8, Moon 2.01e-11, Mars 4.92e-8) — the tail code is inert without a comet (anyActive() false → drawTails early-returns; no comet in the scenes); the significant-dEcl set = the pre-existing distant-TNO STALE class (§11.39). Scene E `scene_e_spine.py` **rc=0, 13/13** (the first run's single `e_in` miss = the documented launch-jd AoI-latch flap, ref=Sun→Earth, resolved 13/13 on re-run — NOT the port; the anyActive() tail gate did NOT hang scene E). **Validation layer POSITIVELY confirmed** [`VK_LOADER_DEBUG=layer`: "Insert instance layer VK_LAYER_KHRONOS_validation"]; **ZERO VUID** across A–D, E, and ALL tail/loader/rare-path operations. Config restored FISHEYE / init_fov 180 (config.ini md5 byte-identical to backup 03fbee59…; re-verified fresh at 180: median dEcl 6.22e-10, drive rc=0, 0 VUID). No data files touched.

The tail has NO on/off flag (a comet always shows its tail) — "flag toggle" maps to hide/show + path-pin. Artifacts T5_rare_{hideshow,timejump,afterdrop}.png.

The "untouched" claim now reads "photometrically inert".]**

46. **Texture-skin seam DUAL (S6 Textures row closed) + tesselation audit correction — 2026-07-19 beta/consolidation run (Claude Fable 5).** Closes the §9 Textures "second pass" remainder — the last non-suspended OLD-ONLY capability class in the T7 gap table.     - **Mechanism**: old `Body::switchMapSkin/createTexSkin` = a `tex_current` pointer swap consumed by every old body class at its map bindings. New = per-module skin state: `BasicMesh` gains a binding-state key (`texBinding`: plain map / big-texture mapping / BIND_SKIN) with ONE rebind site (`bindColor` — was 3 copy-pasted set-rebind blocks; the skin state would have made 5); `LayeredMesh` swaps the DAY layer only (old tex_current feeds exactly the day/base bindings of the layered shaders) via `dayTex()` resolution inside its existing `rebind`, forced through the big-mapping sentinel (0xFFFF) on any state transition. Every transition (on/off, load completion, replace, big appear/drop) lands on one compare per draw entry.
    - **Contract parity (old, exactly)**: create/REPLACE never activates (old resets tex_current to map on replace); activation requires an existing skin (switch(true) skinless = no-op); command surface `body name X skin_tex <file>` / `skin_use on|off|toggle` via coreLink→factory (the "selected" resolution stays in coreLink). Getter stays old-authority pre-switchover (dual-write keeps states agreeing by construction; new-path getter lands at removal).

    - **Beta-state summary (what this run establishes)**: §12 module surface complete except S4-gated rows; S6 seams complete except ledger-suspended items (COLOR authority A3, view_offset A24, sky-lock A25) and retire-with-old classes; scenes A–D+E green, zero VUID, 13 clean shutdowns; the switchover step (§7.8) remains blocked ONLY by the suspended decisions that change user-visible semantics (A1, A3, A9) plus Vixy's call on the moment. **[updated 2026-07-21, §11.48: A1 is now answered (→ B11) and A3 is narrowed to one residual; A9 is the only switchover-blocking decision still fully open — its answer was ambiguous, not absent.]** Local install note: /usr/local/bin/spacecrafter on this machine is a stale 2026-07-17 deploy — refresh at will (`make install`), the harness always runs build-claude directly.

48. **USER_QUESTIONS round 1 — answers received, propagated (2026-07-21).** Source: the 27 questions of the 2026-07-20 companion pass, answered inline by the tester and relayed by Vixy; the verbatim answers stay in `USER_QUESTIONS.md` (that file is the raw record, this entry is what each answer *decides*). **Authority boundary, stated so it is not over-read**: an operator answer is decisive on *what the product must do* (§2 R2 — and where it reports what existing shows/scripts do, it is decisive by §2(b) immutability) and is **not** decisive on mechanism, which stays derived. Propagation rule (13.D): the answer resolves the §13 row, never a copy of it; a fully-resolved row is **removed** from §13.A per that table's own rule (it is "what is live") and listed in §13.C; a partly-answered row **stays** with its residual narrowed in place, so the table never shows a closed question as open or an open one as closed.
    - **(a) Resolved outright — row leaves §13.A, implementation row opens in §13.B**: **A1** (Q14) display gates recording, re-enable starts FRESH — stated reason: avoid paying for invisible accumulation → B11. **A5** (Q20) *"apparent_magnitude and slope can be forced in some cases to fit the appearance"* → physical (albedo/radius) is the default route, H/G is an **authoring override that wins when present** — note this is a *fitted* value by intent, and it is legitimate precisely because it is opt-in at the presentation layer (§2(a) two-layer rule: never fit the model, tools may be fitted). **A8** (Q21) actual surface appearance (limb darkening, granulation, spots) **plus** a non-uniform red chromosphere-like glow matching the prominences → the near-surface star family is a real family, not a MESH emissive shader-swap; the I4 home question is answered by the observable → B12. **A10** (Q13) hidden bodies keep updating ("where it is now") → B19. **A11** (Q2) **keep the same absolute direction in the sky** across reference change / free-mode entry+exit — this also *dissolves* the snap-vs-ease sub-question (nothing reorients, so there is nothing to ease) and leaves the mechanism free → B13. **A12** (Q23) correct the axial-orientation data **moon by moon**, Iapetus named explicitly → B14. **A13** (Q7) anchored-stays-anchored **ratified**; the free-flight-only escalation policy stands as implemented (no code change). **A14** (Q8) at anchored galactic altitudes: **solar-system view from very far, anchor kept** ("in case we go back afterward") — no altitude mode switch → B20. **A19** (Q10) *"Yes, I sometimes do jump dates while navigating"* → the launch-jd AoI latch is now a **defect, not a documented flap**; the ~10% seasonal drift and the scene-E `e_in` first-run miss are the same latch → B15. **A20** (Q27) expose `reloadSystem`, **keep current state** (camera + date), do not reset → B16. **A24** (Q3) view_offset is **in use, for tilted dome geometry** → port, not retire → B17. **A25** (Q4) sky-lock is **in use** → port → B18. **A26**'s big-texture threshold (Q25) 20% confirmed — landed behavior ratified, the row's other convergence points are internal and stay open.
    - **(b) Partly answered — row stays in §13.A, residual narrowed**: **A2** (Q26) *"No. If it's not in the data, it will never be activated ever."* — the user-facing TYPE→SLOT grammar is **not wanted**; deduction-from-keys is the whole authoring contract and the explicit-slot call stays internal wiring. Residual = the **reading boundary**: does that sentence also bind the *runtime command* surface (may `flag trails on` activate a feature whose data keys are absent), or only authoring? The two readings differ observably and one of them constrains every module's `show()`. **A3** (Q15) a "set them all" command **overrides** per-body colors of the kind it names → per-instance storage + broadcast override (a polled global default is excluded: it could not preserve per-body values at all). Residual = does a runtime per-body color survive a body reload. **A4** (Q16) tropics + polar circles come **back**, drawn when the corresponding sky-line flags are on (so the old flag coupling was deliberate, not accidental wiring). Residual = (c) an independent on/off for the grid itself (it rides the AXIS flag today), and whether the old >10 km near-surface-only appearance regime returns. **A9** (Q1) answer reads *"(a) is better"* against a two-case question whose options were listed parent-first in both cases — **ambiguous between "case (a)" and "option (a)"**; recorded UNRESOLVED rather than guessed (the parent-retarget reading is the likelier one and is *not* acted on until confirmed — this is exactly the class §5.2 exists for). **A15** (Q9) **cross-fade requested** at the system-collapse threshold, "if possible without costing too much" → B22; the threshold values themselves were not judged (a tester cannot answer that without seeing them side by side — re-ask deferred until there is something to look at, which is the honest ordering). **A17** (Q11) *"If a body can be seen, we must be able to select it"* — visibility, not system membership, is the selection domain. Residual = the disambiguation rule they added in the same breath: *"the brightest/biggest/main object"* is **three criteria that disagree** (a big dim moon vs a small bright one vs the hierarchy parent) — which arbitrates. **A18** (Q6) free descent aims at **the surface point you are looking at**, not the body centre → B21; residual = the far-descent case (aim at the selected body or land in the vicinity), unanswered. **A27** (Q18/Q19) the "moon" generalisation on non-Earth references is **accepted** (*"Old behavior has no way to be kept"* — an A3-class win confirmed by the user); landscape behavior stated as **auto-select on arrival, manual override then sticks**, which answers the *behavior* but not the row's question (does the association live in the body's data), and leaves "sticks until when" open. **A28** (Q17) **the question failed** — see (d). **A29** (Q24) *"Some bodies can be linked to Earth or Moon or Sun behavior to do some comparisons"* → decisive in direction and it is exactly §2(a2): the special behavior must become a **declarable capability**, never an `englishName` test. Residual = the enumeration (which behaviors are in that set), which is the whole implementable content.
